### ⚡ भविष्य में 1-क्लिक बैकअप लेने के लिए स्क्रिप्ट:
आगे कभी भी नया बैकअप लेने के लिए आपके प्रोजेक्ट में [`scripts/backup_db.ps1`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/scripts/backup_db.ps1) स्क्रिप्ट बना दी गई है। आप टर्मिनल में बस यह कमांड चला सकते हैं:

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\scripts\backup_db.ps1
```

### <a id="turn-3"></a>Turn 3: User Request

> **User:**
>
> script run krne pe ye error aarha pgsql me 
> ERROR:  syntax error at or near "\"
> LINE 5: \restrict uQWjS2SXD7Xb7CybU6NizvoJcttKEnC8kkKelqUQ34izaLQOXH...
>         ^ 
> 
> SQL state: 42601
> Character: 36

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

### यह एरर क्यों आया?

PostgreSQL 17 और 18 के `pg_dump` टूल में सुरक्षा कारणों से बैकअप फाइल की शुरुआत और अंत में दो बैकस्लैश कमांड्स जुड़ते हैं:
- Line 5: `\restrict <key>`
- End line: `\unrestrict <key>`

ये **`psql` (कमांड लाइन)** के मेटा-कमांड्स हैं, स्टैंडर्ड SQL नहीं। जब आप पूरी `.sql` फाइल को **pgAdmin के Query Tool**, **DBeaver** या किसी अन्य SQL एडिटर में पेस्ट करके Execute (F5) करते हैं, तो PostgreSQL का SQL पार्सर बैकस्लैश (`\`) को पहचान नहीं पाता और यह सिंटैक्स एरर थ्रो करता है:
```text
ERROR: syntax error at or near "\"
LINE 5: \restrict ...
```

---

### समाधान (Fix Applied):

1. **मौजूदा बैकअप फाइल्स फिक्स कर दी गई हैं:**
   - [`vargshala_latest_backup.sql`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/backups/vargshala_latest_backup.sql)
   - और [`backups/`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/backups) की सभी फाइल्स में `\restrict` और `\unrestrict` वाली लाइन्स को SQL कमेंट (`--`) में बदल दिया गया है ताकि pgAdmin इसे इग्नोर कर दे।

2. **स्क्रिप्ट [`backup_db.ps1`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/scripts/backup_db.ps1) को हमेशा के लिए ऑटो-फिक्स कर दिया गया है:**
   - स्क्रिप्ट में पोस्ट-प्रोसेसिंग जोड़ दी गई है, जिससे भविष्य में जब भी नया बैकअप बनेगा, वह ऑटोमैटिकली इन दोनों लाइन्स को कमेंट आउट कर देगा।

---

### अब आप क्या करें:

pgAdmin के Query Tool में [`vargshala_latest_backup.sql`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/backups/vargshala_latest_backup.sql) फाइल को दोबारा खोलें (या रीलोड करें) और **Execute (F5)** दबाएँ। अब यह बिना किसी एरर के पूरी तरह से एग्जीक्यूट हो जाएगा।

यहाँ पूरे आर्किटेक्चर और सभी संबंधित फाइल्स का पूरा विवरण है:

### 1. 🏛️ Domain Layer (डेटाबेस मॉडल व स्कीमा)

```text
[PostgreSQL Database (Neon / Local)]
```

#### 3. डेटा मैपिंग और नेविगेशन सेफ्टी (Null-Safety)
- **डबल टेनेंट फ़िल्टर:** रिपॉजिटरी कॉल के बाद `bs.Batch?.Class?.Branch?.OrganizationId == orgId.Value` चेक करता है, जिससे किसी अन्य कोचिंग संस्थान (Tenant) का बैच डेटा लीक होने की कोई संभावना नहीं रहती।
- **Null-Safety:** सभी नेविगेशन्स (`Batch`, `Class`, `Subject`, `Branch`) पर null-conditional (`?.`) और null-coalescing (`?? string.Empty`) लगा हुआ है, जिससे रनटाइम पर `NullReferenceException` आने का कोई चांस नहीं है।
- **सक्रिय व पुरानी एनरोलमेंट्स:** DTO में `IsPrimary`, `EnrollmentType`, `JoinedAt`, `LeftAt`, और `IsActive` सभी फील्ड्स सही से मैप हो रहे हैं, ताकि UI (`Students.razor`) छात्र के एक्टिव और पास्ट दोनों बैचेस को आसानी से दिखा या फ़िल्टर कर सके।

#### 2. [`BatchRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/BatchRepository.cs) में `organizationId` पास करके सीधे SQL में फ़िल्टर करें:
```csharp
public async Task<List<BatchStudent>> GetBatchesByStudentIdAsync(Guid studentId, Guid organizationId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
{
    return await _db.BatchStudents
        .AsNoTracking()
        .Include(bs => bs.Batch)
            .ThenInclude(b => b.Class)
                .ThenInclude(c => c.Branch)
        .Include(bs => bs.Batch)
            .ThenInclude(b => b.Subject)
        .Where(bs => bs.StudentId == studentId 
                  && !bs.Batch.IsDeleted 
                  && bs.Batch.Class.Branch.OrganizationId == organizationId)
        .OrderByDescending(bs => bs.JoinedAt)
        .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);
}
```

#### 3. तब यह [`GetStudentBatchesQueryHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/OrgAdmin/Students/Queries/GetStudentBatches/GetStudentBatchesQueryHandler.cs) सिर्फ 3 साफ़ लाइनों का रह जाएगा:

```csharp
var dtos = batchStudents.Select(bs => bs.ToEnrollmentDto()).ToList();

return ApiResponse<List<StudentBatchEnrollmentDto>>.SuccessResponse(dtos);
```

### मुख्य बिंदु:
1. **Existing Tables Only**: कोई नई table नहीं जोड़ी जाएगी; existing `BatchTeachers` table (`12_BatchTeachers.sql`) का उपयोग होगा।
2. **Branch Filtering & Authorization**: जब बैच में teacher assign करेंगे, तो सिर्फ़ उस बैच की branch के **active teachers** दिखेंगे। Backend handler में भी branch authorization enforce होगी।
3. **Duplicate Prevention & Reactivation**:
   - पहले से assigned active teachers dropdown में नहीं दिखेंगे और backend में भी duplicate active check रहेगा।
   - Remove करने पर रिकॉर्ड physically delete नहीं होगा, बल्कि **soft-disable** (`IsActive = false`, `RemovedAt = UtcNow`) होगा।
   - यदि उसी teacher को दोबारा उसी batch में assign किया जाए, तो वही record **reactivate** हो जाएगा, जिससे PostgreSQL `UNIQUE (BatchId, TeacherId)` constraint violation कभी नहीं होगी।
4. **Multi-Teacher & Multi-Batch Support**: एक batch में multiple teachers और एक teacher multiple batches में assign हो सकेंगे।
5. **Attendance Untouched**: Attendance module में कोई बदलाव नहीं किया जाएगा।
6. **Theme 4 Deep Teal UI**: Clean, responsive, zero-blinking UI design standards के साथ `Batches.razor` में "+ Assign Teacher" flow integrate होगा।

कृपया plan को review करें। आपके अनुमोदन (approval) के बाद मैं तुरंत implementation शुरू करूँगा।

### <a id="turn-14"></a>Turn 14: User Request

> **User:**
>
> @[d:\Rishabhks45\vargshala\src\Vargshala.Web\Vargshala.Web.csproj:L1-L27] isme set kr do auto build

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

[`src/Vargshala.Web/Vargshala.Web.csproj`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Vargshala.Web.csproj) mein **Tailwind CSS ka Auto-Build completely configure aur verify kar diya hai!** 🚀

---

### ❓ Pehle kyu nahi chal raha tha?
`.csproj` mein target already likha hua tha (`Condition="Exists('$(ProjectDir)tailwindcss.exe')"`), lekin disk par `tailwindcss.exe` file missing thi (`.gitignore` mein hone ki wajah se). Isliye MSBuild us target ko silently skip kar deta tha.

---

### ✅ Ab kya kiya gaya hai:
1. **Standalone Compiler Placed**: `tailwindcss.exe` (v3.4.17) ko `src/Vargshala.Web/` directory mein place kar diya gaya hai.
2. **Auto-Download Fallback Added**: `.csproj` mein `EnsureTailwindCompiler` target add kar diya hai — agar future mein kisi nayi machine ya fresh clone par `tailwindcss.exe` na ho, to `dotnet build` use khud background mein auto-download kar lega.
3. **Auto-Compile on Every Build**:
   ```xml
   <!-- Auto-compile Tailwind CSS on dotnet build -->
   <Target Name="EnsureTailwindCompiler" BeforeTargets="BuildTailwindCss" Condition="!Exists('$(ProjectDir)tailwindcss.exe')">
     <Message Importance="high" Text="Tailwind compiler not found. Auto-downloading standalone tailwindcss.exe..." />
     <Exec Command="curl.exe -s -L -o &quot;$(ProjectDir)tailwindcss.exe&quot; &quot;https://github.com/tailwindlabs/tailwindcss/releases/download/v3.4.17/tailwindcss-windows-x64.exe&quot;"
           WorkingDirectory="$(ProjectDir)"
           ContinueOnError="true" />
   </Target>

   <Target Name="BuildTailwindCss" BeforeTargets="BeforeBuild" Condition="Exists('$(ProjectDir)tailwindcss.exe')">
     <Message Importance="high" Text="Building Tailwind CSS..." />
     <Exec Command="&quot;$(ProjectDir)tailwindcss.exe&quot; -i &quot;$(ProjectDir)Styles\app.css&quot; -o &quot;$(ProjectDir)wwwroot\app.css&quot; $(TailwindMinifyFlag)"
           WorkingDirectory="$(ProjectDir)"
           ContinueOnError="true" />
   </Target>

   <PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Configuration)' == 'Release'">
     <TailwindMinifyFlag>--minify</TailwindMinifyFlag>
   </PropertyGroup>
   ```

---

### ⚡ Result:
Ab aap chahe:
- Visual Studio mein **F5** ya **Ctrl + Shift + B** dabayein, ya
- Terminal se `dotnet build` run karein

Tailwind CSS **automatically compile** hoga:
- **Debug Mode**: Superfast (`~880ms` mein build).
- **Release Mode**: Automatically minified bundle (`--minify`).

Aapko manual command run karne ki ab koi zaroorat nahi padegi!

### <a id="turn-23"></a>Turn 23: User Request

> **User:**
>
> Date-wise Attendance Report,Student-wise Attendance Report
> data not load only showing loading

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Searching for `GetDateWiseReport` definitions and calls.

Build verification running for `Attendance.razor` report loading fixes.

**Date-wise Attendance Report** aur **Student-wise Attendance Report** ka loading me atke rehne ka issue fix ho gaya hai:

### Root Cause:
1. **Fire-and-forget without Re-render**: `SetActiveTab` method `void` tha aur `_ = ReloadDateWiseReportAsync();` ko fire-and-forget call kar raha tha. Click event handler turant end ho jata tha, jisse initial render me `_isLoading = true` set hokar shimmer skeleton dikhta tha.
2. **Missing State Notification**: Jab background API call complete hoti thi, tab na to `await` tha aur na hi `InvokeAsync(StateHasChanged)`. Is wajah se Blazor ka UI re-render kabhi trigger hi nahi hota tha aur table hamesha shimmer skeleton (loading state) me hi atka rehta tha.
3. **Missing Batch Cache Reset**: Batch change hone par previous batch ke cached reports clear nahi ho rahe the.
4. **Empty Batch Handling**: Agar koi batch select nahi tha, to loading skeleton ke bajay proper helpful guide message nahi dikh raha tha.

---

### Solved & Applied:
* **Async Tab Switching**: `SetActiveTab` ko `SetActiveTabAsync` me convert karke `await ReloadDateWiseReportAsync()` aur `await ReloadStudentWiseReportAsync()` ke saath wire kiya.
* **Guaranteed UI Update**: Dono report loading methods me `try...finally` ke andar `await InvokeAsync(StateHasChanged);` add kiya, jisse API response aate hi UI turant actual data render karta hai.
* **Gold Standard Table Loading**: Skeletons ab sirf initial fetch par aate hain (`@if (_isLoadingDateReports && !_dateReports.Any())`). Refresh ke dauran smooth `opacity-60` transition rehti hai, jisse header aur columns kabhi jump nahi karte.
* **Batch Cache Invalidation**: Jab user dusra batch select karta hai, purana cached report data auto-clear hokar fresh data load hota hai.
* **Descriptive Empty State**: Batch select na hone par helpful message ("Please select a batch above...") render hota hai.

Build verify ho chuka hai: **0 Errors, 0 Warnings**.

Architecture, Database aur UI teeno jagah isko fully support kiya gaya hai:

1. **Database Level (`BatchTeachers` Table)**:
   - Unique constraint sirf composite pair `(BatchId, TeacherId)` par hai, na ki akele `TeacherId` par.
   - Iska matlab ek hi teacher (`TeacherId`) alag-alag batches (`Batch 1`, `Batch 2`, `Batch 3`) me multiple rows create kar sakta hai.

2. **Backend API & Business Logic**:
   - Backend sirf ye check karta hai ki wo teacher **usi specific batch** me pehle se active to nahi hai (`Teacher is already assigned to this batch`).
   - Dusre batches me us teacher ko assign karne par koi restriction nahi hai.

3. **UI / Frontend Flow**:

Jab aap Teacher ko Batch 1 me assign karte hain, fir Batch 2 me assign karte hain, toh Batch 2 ka subject ID Batch 1 ke mapping ko overwrite kar deta tha. Jab aap Batch 1 par wapas aate the, toh wo teacher Batch 1 ke cards se **gayab** ho jata tha, jisse aisa lagta tha ki ek teacher ek hi batch me assign ho sakta hai.

   Left panel ("Available Teachers") me teacher par sirf static grey badge `Assigned` dikhta tha aur unke total active batches ka count display nahi hota tha.

---

### Kya Problem Thi?
1. **Dictionary Overwrite Bug ([`AssignBatchTeachers.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/AssignBatchTeachers.razor))**:
   - `_teacherSubjectMap` ko `Dictionary<Guid, Guid>` (key: `TeacherId`) banaya gaya tha.
   - Jab aap Teacher ko **Batch A** me assign karte the, fir **Batch B** me assign karte the, toh Batch B ka subject ID Batch A ke subject mapping ko overwrite kar deta tha.
   - Jab aap Batch A par wapas aate the, toh wo teacher Batch A ke subject cards se **gayab** ho jata tha, jisse aisa lagta tha ki ek teacher ek hi batch me reh sakta hai.
2. **Ambiguous Badges & Missing Count**:
   - Left panel ("Available Teachers") me teacher card par sirf static grey badge `Assigned` dikhta tha.

3. **`TeacherDto.Batches` Not Loaded**:
   - Backend se active `BatchTeachers` query me include nahi the, isliye `TeacherDto.BatchCount` hamesha `0` rehta tha.

### Key Improvements Implemented:

1. **Composite Key Subject Mapping ([`AssignBatchTeachers.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/AssignBatchTeachers.razor))**:
   - `_teacherSubjectMap` ko ab `Dictionary<(Guid BatchId, Guid TeacherId), Guid>` kar diya gaya hai.

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

**Haan, bilkul! Real-world coaching aur school management me aisa aksar hota hai** — jaise ek hi faculty member ek hi batch me **Physics aur Chemistry** dono padha sakta hai, ya junior classes me **Maths aur Science** dono.

---

### Abhi Current System me kya ho raha hai?

1. **Database Schema**:
   - Database table `BatchTeachers` me abhi `(BatchId, TeacherId)` ka composite unique index hai.
   - Isme teacher ka batch se direct mapping hai, lekin `BatchTeacher` table ke andar alag se `SubjectId` column nahi hai.
2. **Current UI Behavior ([`AssignBatchTeachers.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/AssignBatchTeachers.razor))**:
   - Abhi jab aap ek teacher ko usi batch me doosre subject card (jaise Chemistry) par drag/drop karte hain:
     - Backend error deta hai: *"Teacher is already assigned to this batch"*.
     - UI par teacher pehle subject se move ho jata hai, dono me ek saath nahi dikhta.
3. **Daily Routine & Sessions ([`ClassSessions.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/ClassSessions.razor))**:
   - Routine aur timetable me `ClassSession` har class ke liye alag se `SubjectId` aur `TeacherId` leti hai, isliye wo teacher us batch me alag-alag din Physics aur Chemistry dono ki class le sakte hain.

### <a id="turn-29"></a>Turn 29: User Request

> **User:**
>
> give me query i will execute

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Verifying compilation of Vargshala.Web...

Verifying build...

Ye lijiye database me execute karne ke liye exact SQL script:

```sql
-- =========================================================================
-- Step 1: BatchTeachers table me SubjectId column add karein (agar nahi hai)
-- =========================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
        WHERE table_name = 'BatchTeachers' AND column_name = 'SubjectId'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE "BatchTeachers" ADD COLUMN "SubjectId" uuid;
        
        -- Step 2: Purane sabhi records ko unke respective Batch ke SubjectId se backfill karein
        UPDATE "BatchTeachers" bt 
        SET "SubjectId" = b."SubjectId" 
        FROM "Batches" b 
        WHERE bt."BatchId" = b."Id";

        -- Step 3: NOT NULL constraint lagayein
        ALTER TABLE "BatchTeachers" ALTER COLUMN "SubjectId" SET NOT NULL;
    END IF;
END $$;

-- =========================================================================
-- Step 4: Purana unique constraint/index (BatchId, TeacherId) drop karein
-- =========================================================================
ALTER TABLE "BatchTeachers" DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS "UQ_BatchTeachers_BatchId_TeacherId";
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "UQ_BatchTeachers_BatchId_TeacherId";

-- =========================================================================
-- Step 5: Naya composite unique index (BatchId, TeacherId, SubjectId) banayein
-- Isse ek teacher ek hi batch me alag-alag subjects me assign ho sakega!
-- =========================================================================
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "UQ_BatchTeachers_BatchId_TeacherId_SubjectId";
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "UQ_BatchTeachers_BatchId_TeacherId_SubjectId" 
    ON "BatchTeachers" ("BatchId", "TeacherId", "SubjectId");

-- =========================================================================
-- Step 6: SubjectId par index create karein (Fast lookup ke liye)
-- =========================================================================
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS "IX_BatchTeachers_SubjectId";
CREATE INDEX "IX_BatchTeachers_SubjectId" ON "BatchTeachers" ("SubjectId");

-- =========================================================================
-- Step 7: Subjects table ke sath Foreign Key link karein
-- =========================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname = 'FK_BatchTeachers_Subjects_SubjectId'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE "BatchTeachers" 
            ADD CONSTRAINT "FK_BatchTeachers_Subjects_SubjectId" 
            FOREIGN KEY ("SubjectId") REFERENCES "Subjects" ("Id") ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;
END $$;
```

---

### Is Query me kya ho raha hai:
1. **Existing Data Safe**: Pehle `SubjectId` nullable add hota hai aur sabhi purane records me unke batch ka `SubjectId` auto-fill hota hai.
2. **Multi-Subject Enabled**: Purana unique index `(BatchId, TeacherId)` drop karke naya unique index `(BatchId, TeacherId, SubjectId)` lagaya gaya hai.
3. **Foreign Key Integrity**: `Subjects` table ke sath foreign key relationship banata hai.

> [!NOTE]
> Local database (`localhost:5432/vargshala`) par ye migration already safely execute aur verify ho chuka hai (sabhi 7 existing records intact hain). Agar aap remote/Neon/production database use kar rahe hain, to upar diya gaya query wahan directly run kar sakte hain.
>
> Saath hi Blazor UI (`AssignBatchTeachers.razor`), MediatR Handlers, Repository aur DTOs sabhi compile ho kar 0 errors ke sath ready hain.

```sql
-- =========================================================================
-- Insert 10 New Subjects for the Current Organization
-- (Automatically picks your active OrganizationId)
-- =========================================================================
WITH current_org AS (
    SELECT "Id" AS org_id 
    FROM "Organizations" 
    ORDER BY "CreatedAt" ASC 
    LIMIT 1
)
INSERT INTO "Subjects" (
    "Id", 
    "OrganizationId", 
    "Name", 
    "Code", 
    "Description", 
    "IsActive", 
    "CreatedAt", 
    "IsDeleted"
)
SELECT 
    gen_random_uuid(), 
    org_id, 
    subj.name, 
    subj.code, 
    subj.description, 
    true, 
    NOW(), 
    false
FROM current_org
CROSS JOIN (
    VALUES
        ('Chemistry',          'CHEM-01', 'General and Organic Chemistry'),
        ('Biology',            'BIO-01',  'Botany and Zoology'),
        ('English',            'ENG-01',  'English Language and Literature'),
        ('Computer Science',   'CS-01',   'Computer Science and Programming Fundamentals'),
        ('Social Studies',     'SST-01',  'History, Civics and Geography'),
        ('Economics',          'ECO-01',  'Micro and Macro Economics'),
        ('Accountancy',        'ACC-01',  'Financial Accounting and Bookkeeping'),
        ('Business Studies',   'BST-01',  'Principles of Management and Business'),
        ('Logical Reasoning',  'REAS-01', 'Logical Reasoning and Quantitative Aptitude'),
        ('Hindi',              'HIN-01',  'Hindi Language and Grammar')
) AS subj(name, code, description)
ON CONFLICT ("OrganizationId", "Code") DO NOTHING;
```

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Maine **Main Branch** aur **Town Hall Campus** dono me **Class 5th se 12th**, **JEE Mains**, **JEE Advanced**, **NEET UG** aur **Foundation** classes database me add kar diye hain.

Agar aapko remote database (Neon / Production) me execute karna ho, to ye raha exact SQL script:

```sql
-- =========================================================================
-- Insert Classes (5th to 12th, JEE Mains, NEET, etc.)
-- for 'Main Branch' and 'Town Hall' across branches
-- =========================================================================
WITH target_branches AS (
    SELECT "Id" AS branch_id, "Name" AS branch_name
    FROM "Branches"
    WHERE "Name" ILIKE '%Main Branch%' OR "Name" ILIKE '%Town Hall%'
),
classes_to_add(name, code, description) AS (
    VALUES
        ('Class 5th',                    'CLS-05',    'Class 5th - Primary Foundation'),
        ('Class 6th',                    'CLS-06',    'Class 6th - Middle School'),
        ('Class 7th',                    'CLS-07',    'Class 7th - Middle School'),
        ('Class 8th',                    'CLS-08',    'Class 8th - Pre-Secondary Foundation'),
        ('Class 9th',                    'CLS-09',    'Class 9th - Secondary Foundation'),
        ('Class 10th',                   'CLS-10',    'Class 10th - Board Preparation'),
        ('Class 11th',                   'CLS-11',    'Class 11th - Higher Secondary Science / Commerce'),
        ('Class 12th',                   'CLS-12',    'Class 12th - Board & Competitive Prep'),
        ('JEE Mains',                    'JEE-MAIN',  'JEE Main Engineering Entrance Preparation'),
        ('JEE Advanced',                 'JEE-ADV',   'JEE Advanced Engineering Entrance Preparation'),
        ('NEET UG',                      'NEET-UG',   'NEET UG Medical Entrance Preparation'),
        ('Foundation (NTSE / Olympiad)', 'FOUND-OLY', 'Junior Olympiad & NTSE Foundation Batch')
)
INSERT INTO "Classes" (
    "Id", 
    "BranchId", 
    "Name", 
    "Code", 
    "Description", 
    "IsActive", 
    "CreatedAt", 
    "IsDeleted"
)
SELECT 
    gen_random_uuid(), 
    b.branch_id, 
    c.name, 
    c.code, 
    c.description, 
    true, 
    NOW(), 
    false
FROM target_branches b
CROSS JOIN classes_to_add c
ON CONFLICT ("BranchId", "Code") DO NOTHING;
```

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Web application ka session timeout aur expiration **3 alag-alag jagaho** par define hai:

---

### 1. Web App Cookie / Session Configuration (Default Policy)
📁 **File:** [`src/Vargshala.Web/Startup.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Startup.cs#L58-L67) *(Lines 58–67)*

```csharp
builder.Services.AddAuthentication(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddCookie(options =>
    {
        options.Cookie.Name = "Vargshala.AuthCookie";
        options.LoginPath = "/login";
        options.LogoutPath = "/account/logout";
        options.AccessDeniedPath = "/login";
        options.ExpireTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromDays(7); // 👉 Default 7 din ka session
        options.SlidingExpiration = true;             // 👉 Active rehne par session extend hota rehta hai
    });
```
- **`ExpireTimeSpan = TimeSpan.FromDays(7)`**: Default session duration **7 din** set hai.
- **`SlidingExpiration = true`**: Agar user web app use kar raha hai to session expire nahi hota, balki time aage badhta rehta hai.

---

### 2. Login Action (Remember Me ke hisab se Dynamic Session)
📁 **File:** [`src/Vargshala.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs#L97-L115) *(Lines 97–115)*

Login ke waqt exact expiration time calculate hota hai:
```csharp
var expiry = rememberMe
    ? DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(7)    // 👉 Agar "Remember Me" checked hai to 7 din (7 Days)
    : DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddHours(24);  // 👉 Agar unchecked hai to 24 ghante (24 Hours)

await HttpContext.SignInAsync(
    CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme,
    new ClaimsPrincipal(identity),
    new AuthenticationProperties
    {
        IsPersistent = rememberMe,
        ExpiresUtc = expiry
    });
```
- **Remember Me Unchecked**: Session **24 ghante (1 din)** me expire ho jayega.
- **Remember Me Checked**: Session **7 din** tak valid rahega.

---

### 3. Backend API Token Expiration (JWT Settings)
📁 **File:** [`src/Vargshala.API/appsettings.json`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.API/appsettings.json#L6-L12) *(Lines 6–12)*

```json
"Jwt": {
  "AccessTokenMinutes": 30,    // 👉 API Access Token: 30 Minutes
  "RefreshTokenDays": 7        // 👉 Refresh Token: 7 Days
}
```
- Background me [`JwtTokenHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Auth/JwtTokenHandler.cs) har 30 minute me access token ko silently refresh karta rehta hai jab tak user ka 7 din ka refresh token / cookie valid hai.

---

2. [`src/Vargshala.Web/Startup.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Startup.cs#L65) me `ExpireTimeSpan` ko change karna hoga.

> Organization Admin:

> Branch Admin:

> - Only data belonging to that branch can be returned or modified.
> - A Branch Admin must never be able to access another branch by changing an ID in the request.

### 🛠️ Jo 4 Sudhar (Fixes) Plan Me Update Kiye Gaye:

1. **Explicit Single-Branch Context for BranchAdmin**:
   - `BranchAdmin` ke session ke liye token generation time par **strictly exactly one active branch** lock hogi (`UserBranchAccesses.FirstOrDefault(a => a.IsActive)`). `BaseBranchAdminController` ye verify karega ki user us branch se actively mapped hai ya nahi (`403 Forbidden` if missing/invalid).
2. **Student Academic Scoping (Corrected)**:
   - Student filtering se `UserBranchAccesses` ko **hata diya gaya hai**.
   - Ab student strictly **academic enrollment chain** se hi filter hoga:
     $$\text{Student} \longrightarrow \text{BatchStudents} \longrightarrow \text{Batch} \longrightarrow \text{Class} \longrightarrow \text{Branch}$$
     `s.BatchStudents.Any(bs => bs.Batch.Class.BranchId == branchId)`.
3. **Mandatory Security Validation on ALL Commands (Not just Queries)**:
   - Sirf GET queries filter nahi hongi; balki **Create, Update, aur Delete Commands par pre-execution branch ownership check** mandatory hoga:
     - **Create**: Class/Batch/Session create karte waqt parent entity (`ClassId`/`BatchId`) ka branch ownership verify hoga, aur `BranchId` server-side token se force-set hoga.
     - **Update & Delete**: Existing record ko pehle verify kiya jayega ki wo relationship chain se authenticated `branchId` me belong karta hai ya nahi. Agar doosri branch ka hai to turant `403 Forbidden` return hoga.
4. **Zero Frontend Trust on BranchId**:
   - Frontend agar request body me `{ "branchId": "OTHER-ID" }` ya query string me `?branchId=...` bhej bhi dega, to backend use **completely overwrite** karke authenticated token ka `BranchId` enforce karega.

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Aapka point bilkul 100% correct aur critical hai!

Agar kisi `BranchAdmin` user ke liye galti se **0** ya **more than 1 active branch mappings** exist karti hain, to silently pehli branch chunna bahut dangerous ho sakta hai (admin socehega wo Branch A par kaam kar raha hai jabki records Branch B me ja rahe honge).

Isliye humne **Fail-Fast & Zero-Ambiguity Rule** enforce kar diya hai:

---

### 🛑 Fail-Fast Enforcement Logic:

1. **Login Time Par ([`LoginCommandHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/Authentication/Commands/Login/LoginCommandHandler.cs))**:
   - Jab koi user `BranchAdmin` role se login karega, to system uske active branch mappings count karega:
     - **Count == 0**: Login turant fail hoga ➔
       > *"No active branch is assigned to this Branch Admin account. Please contact your Institute Administrator."*
     - **Count > 1**: Silent pick **KABHI NAHI HOGA**. Login turant fail hoga with clear configuration error ➔
       > *"Configuration Conflict: Multiple active branch assignments detected for this Branch Admin account. A Branch Admin can only be assigned to exactly one active branch. Please contact your Institute Administrator."*
     - **Count == 1**: Exactly 1 branch hone par hi token generate hoga aur session lock hoga.

2. **API Controller Level Par (`BaseBranchAdminController`)**:
   - Har request par check hoga:
     - Agar database me user ki active branches `!= 1` hain, ya token ka `BranchId` us single active DB branch se match nahi karta, to turant **`403 Forbidden`** return hoga.

### Summary of Implementation: Branch Admin Access Isolation

All requirements, security invariants, and UI standards for **Branch Admin Access Isolation** have been implemented and verified.

---

### Key Architectural Invariants Implemented

1. **Zero CQRS Duplication**:
   - The CQRS layer remains unified and shared between **Organization Admin** and **Branch Admin**.
   - Shared MediatR queries ([`GetStudentsPagedQuery`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/OrgAdmin/Students/Queries/GetStudentsPaged/GetStudentsPagedQuery.cs), [`GetAllActiveBatchesQuery`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/OrgAdmin/Batches/Queries/GetAllActiveBatches/GetAllActiveBatchesQuery.cs), [`GetBranchDashboardQuery`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/BranchAdmin/Dashboard/Queries/GetBranchDashboardQuery.cs), etc.) accept an optional `Guid? branchId`.
   - When called by Branch Admin controllers, `branchId` is populated strictly from the authenticated token's claims on the server side.

2. **Single Active Branch Enforcement**:
   - In [`LoginCommandHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/Authentication/Commands/Login/LoginCommandHandler.cs), [`RefreshTokenCommandHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/Authentication/Commands/RefreshToken/RefreshTokenCommandHandler.cs), and [`AuthRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/AuthRepository.cs), the user's `UserBranchAccess` is evaluated upon authentication:
     - **0 active branches** ➔ `403 Forbidden` (`"No active branch assigned. Please contact your organization administrator."`).
     - **> 1 active branches** ➔ `409 Conflict` (`"Multiple active branch assignments detected. Branch administrators must be assigned to exactly one branch."`).

3. **Student Branch Scoping Invariant**:
   - In [`StudentRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src

### Summary
- **Zero unprotected data endpoints**: All business logic and data manipulation controllers require authentication.
- **Role isolation**: Branch Admins, Organization Admins, Teachers, and Platform SuperAdmins cannot access each other's restricted endpoints.
- **Tenant & Branch safety**: The multi-tenancy global query filter (`OrganizationId == CurrentTenantId`) and server-side `BranchId` claim resolution prevent any cross-tenant or cross-branch data leakage.

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

I am checking the compilation and authorization hierarchy for `AttendancesController.cs`.

Line 17 par `[Authorize(Roles = "BranchAdmin,4")]` ke baare me 2 important points hain:

### 1. Kya ye zaroori hai? (Is it required?)
**Nahi, ye redundant (optional) hai.** 
Kyunki `AttendancesController` inherit karta hai [`BaseBranchAdminController`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.API/Controllers/BranchAdmin/BaseBranchAdminController.cs):
```csharp
[ApiController]
[Authorize(Roles = "BranchAdmin,4")]  // <-- Base class me already laga hua hai
public abstract class BaseBranchAdminController : ControllerBase
```
ASP.NET Core me base class par laga `[Authorize]` attribute **automatically sabhi child controllers par inherit ho jata hai**. Isliye:
- `AttendancesController`
- `ClassesController`
- `BatchesController`
- `StudentsController`
- `TeachersController`
- `ClassSessionsController`
- `DashboardController`

Ye saare controllers already **BranchAdmin (Role = 4)** se secured hain. Saath hi har action method me `await ValidateBranchAccessAsync()` bhi run hota hai.

---

### 2. Agar aap ise yahan rakhna chahte hain (If you want to keep it)
Agar aap har controller par explicitly `[Authorize]` dekhna chahte hain, toh koi problem nahi hai, bas file ke top par **using directive** add karna hoga:
```csharp
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
```
Agar `using` nahi hoga toh IDE me `Authorize` red wavy line (error) show karega.

---

### Recommendation (Recommendation)
Aap **Option A** ya **Option B** me se jo chahein choose kar sakte hain:

* **Option A (Clean Architecture - Recommended):** Line 17 ko remove kar dein, kyunki base class (`BaseBranchAdminController`) ise globally handle karti hai.
* **Option B (Explicit):** File ke top par `using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;` add kar dein taaki IDE warning na de.

### 🛡️ Multi-Tenancy & Branch Scoping Rules
- **No Cross-Branch Leaks**: Every page directly consumes the dedicated Branch Admin client services (`IBranchAttendanceClientService`, `IBranchBatchClientService`, `IBranchClassClientService`, etc.).

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

I have updated `BranchAdmin/Classes.razor` to follow the exact `Users.razor` and `Branches.razor` pattern (`StateHasChanged()` ➔ `await Task.Delay(50)` ➔ `_isDrawerOpen = true`) using standard Tailwind classes without any custom CSS. Running compilation verification now.

Build is running in the background.

Koi extra ya custom CSS add nahi kiya gaya hai. Humne project ke standard reference ([`Users.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/ControlPanel/Users.razor) aur [`Branches.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Branches.razor)) ka exact standard pattern use kiya hai.

---

### 🔍 Issue ki wajah aur Fix:
- **Kyu ho rha tha:** Blazor Server me `@if (_showModal)` ke andar DOM me element create hone ke turant baad agar bina delay ke `_isDrawerOpen = true` ho jata hai, to browser element ko directly screen ke andar render kar deta tha (starting `translate-x-full` state render hone ka time nahi milta tha).
- **OrgAdmin / Users.razor ka Solution:**
  1. Standard Tailwind classes:
     - Backdrop: `transition-opacity duration-300 ease-in-out @(_isDrawerOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0")`
     - Drawer: `transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out @(_isDrawerOpen ? "translate-x-0" : "translate-x-full")`
  2. Open hone ka 2-step flow:
     ```csharp
     _showAddEditModal = true;
     _isDrawerOpen = false;
     StateHasChanged();
     await Task.Delay(50); // Browser ko off-screen state render karne ka samay deta hai
     _isDrawerOpen = true;
     StateHasChanged();    // Smooth slide-in trigger hota hai
     ```
  3. Close hone ka flow:
     ```csharp
     _isDrawerOpen = false;
     await Task.Delay(300); // Transition complete hone ke baad unmount karta hai
     _showAddEditModal = false;
     ```

### 🔍 Root Cause & Fix (Institutes.razor Standard)

[`ControlPanel/Institutes.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/ControlPanel/Institutes.razor) me opening aur closing bilkul smoothly bina blink ke isliye hoti hai kyunki wahan 2-step animation flush use kiya gaya hai:

1. **Drawer Open Flow**:
   ```csharp
   _showAddEditModal = true;
   _isDrawerOpen = false;
   StateHasChanged();          // 1. Pehle drawer DOM me off-screen (translate-x-full, opacity-0) render hota hai
   await Task.Delay(50);       // 2. 50ms ka delay browser ko off-screen starting position calculate karne ka time deta hai
   _isDrawerOpen = true;       // 3. Phir flag true hota hai jisse browser 300ms smooth glide transition animate karta hai
   StateHasChanged();
   ```
2. **Instant Open Response**:
   - `Students.razor` aur `Teachers.razor` me API calls (`RegenerateStudentCodeAsync()`, `RegenerateTeacherCodeAsync()`) drawer open hone ke **baad background** me execute hoti hain, jisse button click karte hi drawer turant bina kisi lag ya blink ke slide in hona shuru ho jata hai.
3. **Drawer Close Flow**:
   ```csharp
   _isDrawerOpen = false;
   StateHasChanged();          // Reverse smooth animation trigger (translate-x-full, opacity-0)
   await Task.Delay(300);      // 300ms CSS transition complete hone ka wait karta hai
   _showAddEditModal = false;  // Transition ke baad DOM se unmount karta hai
   StateHasChanged();
   ```

### <a id="turn-68"></a>Turn 68: User Request

> **User:**
>
> jab login kr rha hu without rememberme check kiye or brower kaat ke fir open kr rha to relogin mang rha jabki 24h rahana chahiye

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Verifying compilation of authentication changes...

Browser band karke dubara open karne par relogin mangne ki problem solve kar di gayi hai.

---

### 🔍 Reason (Kyun ho raha tha?):
Pehle [`AccountController.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs) me ye code tha:
```csharp
IsPersistent = rememberMe, // jab rememberMe unchecked tha, ye false ho jata tha
ExpiresUtc = expiry
```
ASP.NET Core me jab `IsPersistent = false` hota hai, to browser ko jo authentication cookie (`Vargshala.AuthCookie`) bheji jaati hai, wo ek **Session Cookie** ban jaati hai (jisme browser ke liye koi `Expires`/`Max-Age` header nahi hota). 
Standard browser behavior ke mutabiq, **jaise hi browser close hota hai, browser session cookie ko turant delete kar deta hai**. Is wajah se browser reopen karne par cookie gayab hoti thi aur app dobara login mangti thi.

---

### 🛠️ Solution Applied:

1. **[`AccountController.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs)**:
   - `IsPersistent = true` explicitly set kiya gaya hai:
     - **Without Remember Me**: Cookie ab browser me **24 hours** tak persistent rahegi (`ExpiresUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddHours(24)`). Ab browser band karke reopen karne par bhi 24 hours tak aap logged-in rahenge.
     - **With Remember Me**: Cookie **7 days** tak persistent rahegi (`ExpiresUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(7)`).
   - `AllowRefresh = true` enable kiya gaya hai.

2. **[`JwtTokenHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Auth/JwtTokenHandler.cs)**:
   - Browser close karke reopen karne ke baad jab pehli API call hoti hai, to in-memory `_tokenCache` ko cookie claims ke JWT token se automatically repopulate kar diya jaata hai.
   - Background token refresh ke waqt cookie ki remaining validity aur persistence property intact rahegi.

3. **[`Startup.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Startup.cs)**:
   - Cookie ko `IsEssential = true`, `HttpOnly = true`, aur `SameSite = SameSiteMode.Lax` ke sath configure kiya gaya hai.

---

### 🧪 Verification:
- `dotnet build src/Vargshala.Web/Vargshala.Web.csproj /t:Compile` — **0 Warnings, 0 Errors**.
- Minified Tailwind CSS successfully recompiled via `tailwindcss.exe`.

### <a id="turn-75"></a>Turn 75: User Request

> **User:**
>
> table query do

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Yeh lijiye aapke ERD diagram ke mutabik **PostgreSQL CREATE TABLE queries**.

Yeh queries **Vargshala ke Clean Architecture & Multi-Tenancy invariants** (har tenant entity me `OrganizationId`, `UUID`, proper foreign keys, status check constraints aur performance indexes) ke saath design ki gayi hain:

---

### 📜 PostgreSQL DDL Script (Execute in order)

```sql
-- Extension ensure karein (UUID generation ke liye)
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. FEE STRUCTURES (Fee Template per Branch / Academic Session)
-- ============================================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "FeeStructures" (
    "Id" UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    "OrganizationId" UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "Organizations"("Id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
    "BranchId" UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "Branches"("Id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
    "Name" VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    "Description" TEXT,
    "TotalAmount" NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
    "AcademicSession" VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- e.g. '2026-2027'
    "IsActive" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit & Soft Delete
    "CreatedBy" UUID,
    "CreatedAt" TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "UpdatedBy" UUID,
    "UpdatedAt" TIMESTAMPTZ,
    "IsDeleted" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    "DeletedBy" UUID,
    "DeletedAt" TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_FeeStructures_Org_Branch" ON "FeeStructures" ("OrganizationId", "BranchId");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_FeeStructures_Session" ON "FeeStructures" ("AcademicSession", "IsActive");


-- ============================================================================
-- 2. STUDENT FEES (Assigned Fee Structure to a Student)
-- ============================================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "StudentFees" (
    "Id" UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    "OrganizationId" UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "Organizations"("Id") ON DEL
<truncated 4536 bytes>
XISTS "IX_Payments_StudentId" ON "Payments" ("StudentId");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_Payments_Org_Date" ON "Payments" ("OrganizationId", "PaymentDate");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_Payments_TxnRef" ON "Payments" ("TransactionReference");


-- ============================================================================
-- 6. PAYMENT ALLOCATIONS (Mapping Payment to Specific Installments)
-- ============================================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "PaymentAllocations" (
    "Id" UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    "PaymentId" UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "Payments"("Id") ON DELETE CASCADE,
    "FeeInstallmentId" UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "FeeInstallments"("Id") ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    "AllocatedAmount" NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL,

    -- Audit
    "CreatedAt" TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,

    CONSTRAINT "CK_PaymentAllocations_Amount" CHECK ("AllocatedAmount" > 0),
    CONSTRAINT "UQ_PaymentAllocations_Unique" UNIQUE ("PaymentId", "FeeInstallmentId")
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_PaymentAllocations_PaymentId" ON "PaymentAllocations" ("PaymentId");
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_PaymentAllocations_InstallmentId" ON "PaymentAllocations" ("FeeInstallmentId");
```

---

### 🔑 Key Design Highlights
1. **Multi-Tenancy Enforced**: `OrganizationId` un tables me add kiya gaya hai jahan direct tenant-filtering (`CurrentTenantId`) ki zarurat hoti hai (`FeeStructures`, `StudentFees`, `Payments`).
2. **Double Allocations Prevented**: `PaymentAllocations` me `("PaymentId", "FeeInstallmentId")` par unique constraint lagaya gaya hai.
3. **Status Check Constraints**: Status values par validation (`Pending`, `Paid`, `PartiallyPaid`, `Overdue`) database level par guard karti hai.
4. **Referential Integrity**: 
   - `StudentFees` delete hone par uske `FeeDiscounts` aur `FeeInstallments` cascade delete honge.
   - Installments jinki payment ho chuki hai (`PaymentAllocations`), unhe accidently delete hone se bachane ke liye `RESTRICT` rule diya gaya hai.

### <a id="turn-83"></a>Turn 83: User Request

> **User:**
>
> koi changes ki need hai table me

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Aapke dwara provide kiye gaye ER Diagram ke mutabiq tables bilkul sahi create ho chuki hain. 

Lekin **real-world Indian Coaching Institute SaaS** aur **Vargshala Multi-tenancy Architecture** ke hisaab se **kuch zaroori (crucial) improvements** ki zaroorat hai, jisse aage chal kar queries slow na ho aur accounting me koi issue na aaye:

---

### 🚨 1. Mandatory Multi-Tenancy Rule (Vargshala Architecture)
Vargshala ke mandatory invariant ke hisaab se:
> *"Every tenant entity MUST have `Guid OrganizationId` for EF Core Global Query Filter (`e => e.OrganizationId == CurrentTenantId`)."*

* **Issue:** Abhi `FeeInstallments` aur `FeeDiscounts` me `OrganizationId` nahi hai (sirf `StudentFeeId` hai). 
* **Recommendation:** Dono tables me **`OrganizationId UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES "Organizations"("Id")`** add karna chahiye, taaki tenant isolation aur EF Core queries direct filter ho sakein bina nested join ke.

---

### 💡 2. Crucial Business Logic Improvements

#### A. `Payments` Table me:
1. **`ReceiptNumber` (Receipt No.)**:
   * *Kyu chahiye:* Har institute me offline ya online fee payment par **Receipt No.** (e.g. `REC-2026-00124`) generate hota hai jise parents ko print ya WhatsApp kiya jata hai. Abhi sirf `TransactionReference` hai (jo bank UTR/UPI ref ke liye hota hai).
   * *Add karein:* `"ReceiptNumber" VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE`
2. **`BranchId`**:
   * *Kyu chahiye:* Branch Admin ko apne branch ka daily cash/online collection report dekhna hota hai ("Ajj Main Branch me kitna cash collect hua?").
   * *Add karein:* `"BranchId" UUID REFERENCES "Branches"("Id")`

#### B. `StudentFees` Table me:
1. **`PaidAmount` & `DueAmount`**:
   * *Kyu chahiye:* Abhi UI par agar "Fee Due: ₹15,000" ya "Paid: ₹35,000" dikhana ho, toh har student ke liye `PaymentAllocations` ko join aur SUM karna padega (heavy query). Agar `StudentFees` me direct `PaidAmount` (Default 0.00) ho, toh dashboard query 10x fast ho jayegi.
   * *Add karein:* `"PaidAmount" NUMERIC(12, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00`

#### C. `FeeInstallments` Table me:
1. **`PaidAmount`**:
   * *Kyu chahiye:* Maan lijiye installment ₹10,000 ki hai aur student ne partial payment ₹4,000 ki. tallments" ("OrganizationId");

```sql
ALTER TABLE public."Payments" 
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "ReceiptNumber" VARCHAR(50) NULL,
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "BranchId" UUID NULL;

DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname = 'UQ_Payments_OrganizationId_ReceiptNumber'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE public."Payments"
            ADD CONSTRAINT "UQ_Payments_OrganizationId_ReceiptNumber" 
            UNIQUE ("OrganizationId", "ReceiptNumber");
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint WHERE conname = 'FK_Payments_Branches_BranchId'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE public."Payments"
            ADD CONSTRAINT "FK_Payments_Branches_BranchId" 
            FOREIGN KEY ("BranchId") 
            REFERENCES public."Branches"("Id") 
            ON UPDATE CASCADE 
            ON DELETE SET NULL;
    END IF;
END $$;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_Payments_ReceiptNumber" 
    ON public."Payments" ("ReceiptNumber");

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS "IX_Payments_BranchId" 
    ON public."Payments" ("BranchId");

COMMIT;
```

```csharp
{

    public const string Active = "Active";
    public const string Inactive = "Inactive";
}

{

    {

        FeeStructureStatus.Active => FeeStructureStatusNames.Active,
        FeeStructureStatus.Inactive => FeeStructureStatusNames.Inactive,
        _ => status.ToString()

    {

        _ => null
    };

    public static FeeStructureStatus FromNullableBool(bool? isActive) => isActive switch
    {

        false => FeeStructureStatus.Inactive,
        null => FeeStructureStatus.All
    };
}
```

### 1. Created Universal Status Enum
[`src/Vargshala.Contracts/Common/EntityStatus.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Contracts/Common/EntityStatus.cs):
```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace Vargshala.Contracts.Common;

/// <summary>
/// Universal operational status for entities, records, and dropdown filters across Vargshala SaaS.
/// </summary>
public enum EntityStatus
{
    [Display(Name = "All Statuses")]
    All = 0,

    [Display(Name = "Active")]
    Active = 1,

    [Display(Name = "Inactive")]
    Inactive = 2
}

public static class EntityStatusNames
{
    public const string All = "All";
    public const string Active = "Active";
    public const string Inactive = "Inactive";
}

public static class EntityStatusExtensions
{
    public static string GetDisplayName(this EntityStatus status) => status switch
    {
        EntityStatus.All => "All Statuses",
        EntityStatus.Active => EntityStatusNames.Active,
        EntityStatus.Inactive => EntityStatusNames.Inactive,
        _ => status.ToString()
    };

    public static bool? ToNullableBool(this EntityStatus status) => status switch
    {
        EntityStatus.Active => true,
        EntityStatus.Inactive => false,
        _ => null
    };

    public static EntityStatus FromNullableBool(bool? isActive) => isActive switch
    {
        true => EntityStatus.Active,
        false => EntityStatus.Inactive,
        null => EntityStatus.All
    };

    public static EntityStatus FromString(string? value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return EntityStatus.All;
        if (bool.TryParse(value, out var b)) return b ? EntityStatus.Active : EntityStatus.Inactive;
        if (Enum.TryParse<EntityStatus>(value, true, out var result)) return result;
        return EntityStatus.All;
    }
}
```

### 🔍 Root Cause

`PagedResponse<T>.Create` ka method signature yeh hai:
```csharp
public static PagedResponse<T> Create(
    List<T> items, 
    int totalRecords,   // 👈 2nd argument totalRecords hai
    int pageNumber,     // 👈 3rd argument pageNumber hai
    int pageSize)
```

Lekin handler me parameters galti se is tarah pass ho gaye the:
```csharp
// ❌ Pehle yeh likha tha (Wrong Order):
var response = PagedResponse<FeeStructureDto>.Create(
    dtos, 
    query.Request.PageNumber, // 👈 Yahan PageNumber (1) pass ho gaya totalRecords ki jagah!
    query.Request.PageSize, 
    totalRecords);
```

Is wajah se:
1. `TotalRecords` ko `1` (`query.Request.PageNumber`) mil gaya.
2. Jabki database me abhi **0** records the (`items = []`).
3. Isliye table me 0 rows thi (*"No fee structures found"*), aur KPI card par `Total Templates` **`1`** dikh raha tha (`0 Active • 0 Inactive`).

---

### ✅ Solution / Fix

[`GetFeeStructuresPagedQueryHandler.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/OrgAdmin/FeeStructures/Queries/GetFeeStructuresPaged/GetFeeStructuresPagedQueryHandler.cs) me parameter order ko theek kar diya gaya hai:

```csharp

    dtos, 
    totalRecords, 
    query.Request.PageNumber, 
    query.Request.PageSize);
```

```csharp
public static class FeeFilterHelper
{
    // StudentFeeStatus enum se dynamic list
    public static List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> GetStatusFilterOptions(string allLabel = "All Status")
    {
        var options = new List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> { new("", allLabel) };
        foreach (var status in Enum.GetValues<StudentFeeStatus>())
            options.Add(new(status.ToString(), status.GetDisplayName()));
        return options;
    }

    // PaymentMethod enum se dynamic list (UPI, Cash, NetBanking, Card, Cheque, DD)
    public static List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> GetPaymentMethodOptions() =>
        Enum.GetValues<PaymentMethod>()
            .Select(m => new CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption(m.ToString(), m.GetDisplayName()))
            .ToList();

    // FeeDiscountType enum se dynamic list (Fixed, Percentage, Scholarship, Sibling)
    public static List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> GetDiscountTypeOptions(string noneLabel = "No Discount")
    {
        var options = new List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> { new("", noneLabel) };
        foreach (var discount in Enum.GetValues<FeeDiscountType>())
            options.Add(new(discount.ToString(), discount.GetDisplayName()));
        return options;
    }

    // Standard Installment plans
    public static List<CustomSelect.CustomSelectOption> GetInstallmentOptions() => ...;
}
```

### Root Cause of the Error

Yeh error is wajah se aa raha tha:
1. Domain layer me `StudentFee` aur `Payment` entities **`BaseEntity`** ko inherit karti hain.
2. `BaseEntity` me `IsActive` property defined hai:
   ```csharp
   public abstract class BaseEntity
   {
       public Guid Id { get; set; }
       public bool IsActive { get; set; } = true;
       // ... CreatedAt, UpdatedAt, IsDeleted, etc.
   }
   ```
3. Jab EF Core `_db.StudentFees` ko query kar raha tha (`GetPagedStudentFeesAsync`), toh woh `BaseEntity` ke saare mapped columns (including `s."IsActive"`) select kar raha tha.
4. Lekin PostgreSQL database me `StudentFees` aur `Payments` tables me `"IsActive"` column create nahi hua tha, jis wajah se PostgreSQL ne error throw kiya:
   > `42703: column s.IsActive does not exist`

---

### Fixes Applied

1. **Database Schema Updated (PostgreSQL)**:
   PostgreSQL database me `StudentFees` aur `Payments` dono tables me missing column add kar diya gaya:
   ```sql
   ALTER TABLE public."StudentFees" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "IsActive" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE;
   ALTER TABLE public."Payments" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "IsActive" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE;
   ```

2. **Entity Configurations Updated**:
   - [`StudentFeeConfiguration.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/StudentFeeConfiguration.cs):
     ```csharp
     builder.Property(sf => sf.IsActive)
         .IsRequired()
         .HasDefaultValue(true);
     ```
   - [`PaymentConfiguration.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/PaymentConfiguration.cs):
     ```csharp
     builder.Property(p => p.IsActive)
         .IsRequired()
         .HasDefaultValue(true);
     ```

3. **Master DDL Updated**:
   - [`Fee_Management.sql`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Domain/Db/Fee_Management.sql) me `StudentFees` aur `Payments` definitions me `"IsActive" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE` include kar diya gaya.

### 1. Error ka Root Cause (`column f2.CreatedBy does not exist`)

Jab Collect Payment command chali, tab `FeeRepository.GetActiveStudentFeeByStudentIdAsync` call hua:
```csharp
return await _db.StudentFees
    .Include(sf => sf.Student).ThenInclude(s => s.User)
    .Include(sf => sf.FeeStructure).ThenInclude(fs => fs.Branch)
    .Include(sf => sf.FeeStructure).ThenInclude(fs => fs.Class)
    .Include(sf => sf.Installments)
    .Include(sf => sf.Discounts) // <-- f2 alias yahan FeeDiscounts ka tha
    .Where(sf => sf.StudentId == studentId && !sf.IsDeleted && sf.Status != "Cancelled")
    .OrderByDescending(sf => sf.AssignedAt)
    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
```

- C# entity [`FeeDiscount.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Domain/Entities/FeeDiscount.cs) me property defined thi: `public Guid? CreatedBy { get; set; }`.
- Lekin PostgreSQL database me `"FeeDiscounts"` table me `"CreatedBy"` column physically create nahi hua tha.
- Is wajah se EF Core ne jab query generate ki (`SELECT ... f2."CreatedBy" FROM "FeeDiscounts" AS f2`), toh PostgreSQL ne error throw kiya:
  > `42703: column f2.CreatedBy does not exist`

**Fix Applied**:
PostgreSQL me `"FeeDiscounts"` aur `"PaymentAllocations"` dono me missing audit column add kar diya gaya:
```sql
ALTER TABLE public."FeeDiscounts" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "CreatedBy" UUID REFERENCES public."Users"("Id") ON DELETE SET NULL;
ALTER TABLE public."PaymentAllocations" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "CreatedBy" UUID REFERENCES public."Users"("Id") ON DELETE SET NULL;
```

### 3. Automatic Tracking via `SaveChangesAsync`

[`VargshalaDbContext.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/VargshalaDbContext.cs) me centralized ChangeTracker hook laga hua hai:
```csharp
foreach (var entry in ChangeTracker.Entries<Domain.Common.BaseEntity>())
{
    switch (entry.State)
    {
        case EntityState.Added:
            entry.Entity.CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            if (entry.Entity.Id == Guid.Empty)
                entry.Entity.Id = Guid.NewGuid();
            break;

        case EntityState.Modified:
            entry.Entity.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            break;
    }
}
```

### Error Cause & Fix: Duplicate Key on `UQ_Payments_OrganizationId_ReceiptNumber`

#### 1. Error ka Mukhya Kaaran (Root Cause):
Payment collect karte waqt receipt number generate karne ke liye ye logic thi:
```csharp
var countThisMonth = await _db.Payments
    .CountAsync(p => p.OrganizationId == orgId && p.ReceiptNumber.StartsWith(prefix));

return $"{prefix}{(countThisMonth + 1):D4}";
```
- Pehle database me seed kiye gaye payments me `REC-202609-0010` aur `REC-202609-0011` pehle se maujood the.
- Jab naya payment collect karne ki koshish ki gayi, tab table me total payments count **9** tha, toh `count + 1 = 10` calculate hua aur system ne dubara **`REC-202609-0010`** generate kar diya.
- Is wajah se unique constraint `"UQ_Payments_OrganizationId_ReceiptNumber"` violate ho gayi aur `DbUpdateException` throw hua.

---

#### 2. Kaha Fix Kiya Gaya:
[`src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs#L214-L245) me `GenerateReceiptNumberAsync` ko collision-free banaya gaya:

```csharp
public async Task<string> GenerateReceiptNumberAsync(Guid orgId, CancellationToken ct = default)
{
    var yearMonth = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("yyyyMM");
    var prefix = $"REC-{yearMonth}-";

    var existingNumbers = await _db.Payments
        .IgnoreQueryFilters()
        .Where(p => p.OrganizationId == orgId && p.ReceiptNumber != null && p.ReceiptNumber.StartsWith(prefix))
        .Select(p => p.ReceiptNumber!)
        .ToListAsync(ct);

    var existingSet = new HashSet<string>(existingNumbers, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    int maxSeq = 0;
    foreach (var r in existingNumbers)
    {
        if (r.Length > prefix.Length && int.TryParse(r.Substring(prefix.Length), out int parsed))
        {
            if (parsed > maxSeq) maxSeq = parsed;
        }
    }

    int nextSeq = Math.Max(existingNumbers.Count + 1, maxSeq + 1);
    string candidate = $"{prefix}{nextSeq:D4}";
    while (existingSet.Contains(candidate))
    {
        nextSeq++;
        candidate = $"{prefix}{nextSeq:D4}";
    }

    return candidate;
}
```

- Ab ye algorithm table ke sabhi existing receipts ka **maximum sequence number** calculate karta hai aur ensure karta hai ki generated candidate number table me pehle se exist na kare.

   - Empty body hone ki wajah se `.NET` ka JSON parser `System.Text.Json.JsonException` throw kar deta tha ki *"koi JSON token nahi mila"*, aur wahi raw exception text aapko toast notification me show ho raha tha.

### 2. Dono Layers Par Fix Kar Diya Gaya Hai:

### "Failed to retrieve student fees (HTTP 401)" Ka Pura Analysis & Fix

Aapke screenshot me jo error dikha:
> **"Failed to retrieve student fees (HTTP 401)"**

Pehle jo error aa raha tha:
> *"The input does not contain any JSON tokens..."*

Ye dono **ek hi cheez** hain! Pehle jab API `401 Unauthorized` bhejti thi, toh response body empty (0 bytes) hoti thi aur JSON parser crash ho raha tha. Jab humne `ParseResponseAsync` add kiya, toh usne asal wajah (real status code) reveal kar di: **HTTP 401 (Session/Token Expired)**.

---

### 1. HTTP 401 Aane Ka Exact Reason:

1. **Test Scripts Se Login:**
   - Pichle steps me payment collection aur statistics test karne ke liye humne background script se `orgadmin@yopmail.com` par login kiya tha.
   - Backend architecture me har login par naya `RefreshToken` generate hota hai aur DB me update ho jata hai.
2. **Browser Session Invalidation:**
   - Is wajah se aapke browser ke cookie me jo purana `RefreshToken` tha, wo database ke naye token se mismatch ho gaya.
   - Saath hi JWT `AccessToken` ki 30-minute validity bhi expire ho chuki thi. Jab browser ne background me token refresh karne ki koshish ki, toh API ne invalid session hone ki wajah se **401 Unauthorized** return kiya.

---

### 2. Code Me Kya Fix Kiya Gaya:

1. **Automatic Session Expire Handling ([`Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Fees.razor)):**
   - Ab agar token expire hota hai ya 401 aata hai, toh page par error toast ke sath automatic `/login` par redirect ho jayega:
   ```csharp
   if (listRes.Message.Contains("401") || listRes.Message.Contains("Unauthorized"))
   {
       Toast.Error("Your session has expired. Please log in again.");
       Navigation.NavigateTo("/login", forceLoad: true);
       return;
   }
   ```
2. **Safe Deserialization ([`FeeService.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Services/FeeService.cs)):**
   - Kisi bhi network failure ya non-JSON response par raw JSON parser crash nahi hoga.

### Summary of Updates

All side drawers and branch selection requirements have been implemented and aligned with the gold-standard design system:

---

### 1. Side Drawer Redesign (Matching [Students.razor](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Students.razor))
All drawers on [`Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Fees.razor) (**Collect Fee Payment**, **Assign Fee Structure**, and **Statement & Installments**) now use the unified gold-standard slide-over drawer system:
- **Backdrop Overlay**: Smooth fade transition (`bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs`, `opacity-100` vs `opacity-0`).
- **Right Slide-Over**: Full-height drawer (`w-full max-w-lg`, `translate-x-0` vs `translate-x-full` with 300ms easing).
- **Theme 4 Deep Teal Header**: `#004D40` background, rounded logo badge (`images/Logo.svg` with border glow), ambient light effect (`#009488/30 blur-2xl`), title, subtitle, and close button.
- **Scrollable Form Body**: `flex-1 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar` with zero visible scrollbar and uppercase teal section headers (`#00796b`).
- **Footer**: `bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#0c1417]` with Cancel and primary gradient button (`from-[#009488] to-[#00796b]`).

---

### 2. In-Drawer Branch Dropdown with Main Branch Pre-Selection
- **Branch Selection Inside Drawers**:
  - A `<CustomSelect>` for **Branch** is now the first field in Section 1 of both the **Collect Fee Payment** and **Assign Fee Structure** drawers.
- **Automatic Main Branch Defaulting**:
  - Handled via `DetermineDefaultDrawerBranchId()`:
    1. Checks active topbar branch context (`BranchContext.CurrentBranchId`).
    2. If logged in as Main Branch or Org Admin without a branch selected, it resolves and defaults to the organization's **Main Branch** (`_branches.FirstOrDefault(b => b.IsMainBranch)`).
    3. If opening "Collect Fee" from a student table row, it automatically pre-selects that student's specific branch and student account.
- **Dynamic Branch Filtering**:
  - Selecting another branch in the drawer resets student/package inputs and triggers `LoadDrawerStudentsAsync(branchGuid)` and `LoadDrawerStructuresAsync(branchGuid)` so that only students and fee packages belonging to the chosen branch are displayed.
- **Backend Branch Association**:
  - [`FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs) (`GetStudentsForFeeAssignmentAsync`) resolves student branch memberships through both `BatchStudents -> Batch -> Class -> Branch` and `User -> UserBranchAccesses`.

### <a id="turn-103"></a>Turn 103: User Request

> **User:**
>
> in dropdown showing double data
> 
> and default selected main branch so show in dropdown only main branch student in drawer

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Wait for the Infrastructure compilation task to finish.

Wait for the Web compilation task to finish.

Wait for the Tailwind CSS compilation to complete.

### Fixes Implemented

#### 1. Eliminated "Double Data" in Class Dropdown
- **Root Cause**: On initial page load, the branch filter was unassigned (`""` / "All Branches"). This caused `ClassService.GetAllActiveClassesAsync(null)` to return classes from **all** branches across the organization. Because both *Main Branch* and *Town Hall Campus* define `Class 10th`, `Class 11th`, and `Class 12th`, each class appeared twice with identical text in the dropdown.
- **Fix**:
  - In [`Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Fees.razor), `_selectedBranchId` now automatically **defaults to the organization's Main Branch** on initial load:
    ```csharp
    if (!keepBranchSelection && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_selectedBranchId))
    {
        var mainBranch = _branches.FirstOrDefault(b => b.IsMainBranch) ?? _branches.FirstOrDefault();
        if (mainBranch != null)
        {
            _selectedBranchId = mainBranch.Id.ToString();
        }
    }
    ```
  - This immediately scopes the Class dropdown to only the classes in the **Main Branch** (`Class 10th`, `Class 11th`, `Class 12th` without any duplicate entries).
  - If a user explicitly selects "All Branches", the Class dropdown labels are cleanly disambiguated with campus tags (e.g. `Class 10th (Main Branch)` and `Class 10th (Town Hall Campus)`) so they never show duplicate identical labels.

---

#### 2. Default Selected Main Branch & Scoped Students in Drawer
- **Drawer Branch Default**:
  - `DetermineDefaultDrawerBranchId()` prioritizes the active page branch selection (which defaults to Main Branch) or `_branches.FirstOrDefault(b => b.IsMainBranch)`.
  - When the **Collect Fee** or **Assign Fee Structure** drawer opens, **Main Branch** is selected by default in the drawer's Branch `<CustomSelect>`.
- **Showing Only Main Branch Students in Drawer**:
  - In `LoadDrawerStudentsAsync(branchGuid)`, the student lookup is strictly filtered to the selected branch:
    ```csharp
    if (branchId.HasValue && branchId.Value != Guid.Empty)
    {
        _allDrawerStudentLookups = _allDrawerStudentLookups
            .Where(s => s.BranchId == branchId.Value || s.BranchId == Guid.Empty)
            .ToList();
    }
    ```
  - In [`FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs) (`GetStudentsForFeeAssignmentAsync`), primary batch and branch metadata resolution is now restricted to the requested branch, ensuring that students only display classes and details relevant to the selected branch.
  - When switching the Branch dropdown in the drawer, the student list and fee structure package dropdowns dynamically reload for that selected branch.

---

### Verification
- **`Vargshala.Infrastructure`**: Compiled cleanly (`0 Errors, 0 Warnings`).
- **`Vargshala.Web`**: Compiled cleanly (`0 Errors, 0 Warnings`).
- **Tailwind CSS**: Rebuilt and minified to [`src/Vargshala.Web/wwwroot/app.css`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/wwwroot/app.css).

#### 🤖 Antigravity AI Response:

Branch-wise student data binding completely test aur verify ho gaya hai.

---

### 🔍 Kyo Double / Sabhi Branch me 11 Students dikh rahe the? (Root Cause)
1. **Synthetic Test Data Conflict**:
   - Ek purane test script ne Town Hall Campus ke 4 test batches (`TH Physics 12`, `TH Math 12`, `TH Foundation 10`, `TH JEE Batch`) me sabhi 11 students ko enroll kar diya tha.
   - Saath hi `Aryan Kapoor` (jo Class 8th Town Hall Campus ka student hai) usko Main Branch ke batches me bhi enroll kiya hua tha.
2. **Repository & UI Querying**:
   - EF Core query har student ke active enrollments check karti hai. Dono branches me sabhi students enrolled hone ki wajah se dono jagah 11 students return ho rahe the.

---

### 🛠️ Kya Fix Kiya Gaya?
1. **Clean Branch Association**:
   - Town Hall Campus ke test batches se synthetic cross-branch enrollments deactivate kiye gaye.
   - Town Hall Campus me sirf genuine student **`Aryan Kapoor`** (`Class 8th`) bind hua.
   - Main Branch me genuine **10 students** (`Aditya`, `Ananya`, `Diya`, `Ishan`, `Kunal`, `Rishabh`, `Rohan`, `Saurav`, `Sneha`, `Tanvi`) bind hue.
2. **Backend Optimization ([`FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs#L185-L225))**:
   - `StudentFees.FeeStructure` ko include kiya taaki active fee aur pending due calculation strictly requested `branchId` ke according ho.
3. **Frontend Dynamic Binding ([`Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Fees.razor#L1361-L1415))**:
   - `HandleDrawerBranchChangedAsync` branch change hone par students aur fee packages ko branch-wise re-fetch karta hai.
   - `LoadDrawerStudentsAsync` me strictly selected branch ke students filter hote hain.

---

Sachchai aur background yeh hai:

### 1. Puraane Session ka Data (Jo Pehle se Database me tha):
- Pichhle session me jab Attendance aur Class Sessions ka data test karne ke liye script (`populate_all_class_sessions.py`) chala tha, tab us script ne Town Hall Campus ke 4 test batches (`TH Physics 12`, `TH Math 12`, etc.) banaye the aur ek blind loop chala kar **saare 11 students ko dono branches ke batches me enroll** kar diya tha.
- Is wajah se jab aap Town Hall Campus select kar rahe the, tab bhi saare 11 students show ho rahe the.

### 2. Maine Abhi Kya Kiya:
- Maine **koi naya mock data add nahi kiya**.
- Balki unhi 4 fake test batches ke cross-enrollments ko **deactivate (`IsActive = false`)** kiya taaki:
  - **Town Hall Campus** me sirf wahi student aaye jo sach me Town Hall ka hai (**`Aryan Kapoor`** - Class 8th).
  - **Main Branch** me Main Branch ke **10 students** aayein.

---

### 3. "Assign Structure button not work" aur `ObjectDisposedException` kyu aaya tha?
Aapko Visual Studio me jo error aaya:
```text
System.ObjectDisposedException: The CancellationTokenSource has been disposed.
at Vargshala.Web.Components.UI.Notifications.AlertNotification.CloseAsync() in AlertNotification.razor:line 147
```
- Jab aapne Assign button click kiya aur Toast message show hua, to **`AlertNotification.razor`** ka 4-second auto-dismiss timer complete hone par `_autoDismissCts.Cancel()` call hua.
- Kyunki woh CTS pehle hi dispose ho chuka tha, .NET ne `ObjectDisposedException` throw kiya aur Visual Studio debugger ne wahi par application ko pause kar diya (jis se Blazor circuit ruk gaya aur button unclickable ho gaya).

### ✅ Yeh Error Fix Kar Diya Gaya Hai:
[`AlertNotification.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/UI/Notifications/AlertNotification.razor) me thread-safe `Interlocked.Exchange` aur safe disposal laga diya gaya hai, taaki toast close hone par kabhi bhi `ObjectDisposedException` na aaye.

Aap Visual Studio me **Continue (F5)** ya **Restart (Ctrl + Shift + F5)** karke test kar sakte hain — Assign Structure button aur Toast smoothly chalenge.

### 🔍 Problem (Root Cause):
1. **Equal z-index clash**: Drawers, backdrops aur modals me `z-50` laga hua tha, aur [`ToastContainer.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/UI/Notifications/ToastContainer.razor) aur [`AlertNotification.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/UI/Notifications/AlertNotification.razor) me bhi sirf `z-50` tha.
2. **DOM Order**: [`MainLayout.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Layout/MainLayout.razor) me `<ToastContainer />` sabse upar (Sidebar `<NavMenu>` aur page `@Body` se pehle) likha hua tha. CSS ke rule ke mutabiq jab do elements ka z-index barabar (`50`) hota hai, toh baad me aane wala element (Drawer/Sidebar) pehle aane wale element (Toast) ke **upar** aa jata tha. Is wajah se drawer open hone par notification drawer ke peeche chhip jata tha.

---

### 🛠️ Key Fixes:
1. **Top-Level `z-index: 99999`**:
   - [`ToastContainer.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/UI/Notifications/ToastContainer.razor): Container aur items ko `z-[99999]` aur inline `style="z-index: 99999;"` de diya gaya hai.
   - [`AlertNotification.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/UI/Notifications/AlertNotification.razor): Floating state me `z-[99999]` aur `style="z-index: 99999;"` apply kar diya gaya hai.
2. **DOM Stacking Order Updated**:
   - [`MainLayout.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Layout/MainLayout.razor): `<ToastContainer />` ko sidebar aur main body ke baad layout ke **aakhri child** me move kar diya gaya hai, taaki yeh naturally har cheez ke upar render ho.
   - [`AuthLayout.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Layout/AuthLayout.razor): Isme bhi `<ToastContainer />` ko bottom me shift kiya gaya hai.

Aapka observation bilkul sahi tha! Main Branch ka data Branch Admin page par kyun show ho raha tha aur ise kaise solve kiya gaya hai:

---

### 🔍 Main Branch Data Show Hone Ka Root Cause:
1. **Mock Test Data**: 
   Pehle run kiye gaye ek test script (`seed_sample_student_fees.py`) ne 4 students:
   - **Aditya Joshi** (`STU-26004`)
   - **Saurav Sharma** (`VI-2300545`)
   - **Rishabh Sharma** (`STU-2600001`)
   - **Ananya Deshmukh** (`STU-26003`)
   
   ko test fee assign ki thi. Lekin us script ne Fee Structure `Physics` select kiya tha jo **Town Hall Campus** ka tha, jabki ye chaaro students **Main Branch** (`Class 10th`, `JEE Mains`, `NEET UG`) ke hain!
2. **Query Filter Gap**: 
   [`FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs) ke `GetPagedStudentFeesAsync` me sirf `sf.FeeStructure.BranchId == branchId` check tha. Student kis branch me enrolled hai ye cross-verify nahi ho raha tha, isliye Town Hall Campus ke page par ye Main Branch ke students show ho rahe the.

---

### 🛠️ Kya Fix Kiya Gaya:

#### 1. Database Mock Data Clean-up
- Un 4 synthetic mock records (`Aditya Joshi`, `Saurav Sharma`, `Rishabh Sharma`, `Ananya Deshmukh`) ke mock StudentFees, installments aur dummy payment allocations ko database se cleanly remove/soft-delete kar diya gaya hai.

#### 2. Strict Multi-Tenancy & Branch Scoping ([`FeeRepository.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FeeRepository.cs))
- `GetPagedStudentFeesAsync` aur `GetFeeStatisticsAsync` dono queries me strict dual-validation lagayi gayi hai:
  ```csharp
  if (branchId.HasValue && branchId.Value != Guid.Empty)
  {
      query = query.Where(sf =>
          (sf.FeeStructure != null && sf.FeeStructure.BranchId == branchId.Value) &&
          (sf.Student.BatchStudents.Any(bs => bs.IsActive && !bs.Batch.IsDeleted && !bs.Batch.Class.IsDeleted && bs.Batch.Class.BranchId == branchId.Value) ||
           sf.Student.User.UserBranchAccesses.Any(uba => uba.IsActive && uba.BranchId == branchId.Value)));
  }
  ```
  Ab kisi bhi haal me kisi dusri branch ka student kisi aur branch me display nahi hoga.

#### 3. Controller Security ([`BranchAdmin/FeesController.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.API/Controllers/BranchAdmin/FeesController.cs))
- `AssignFee`, `CollectFee`, aur `GetDetails` endpoints par student aur fee structure dono ka branch-level verification add kar diya gaya hai, taaki cross-branch fee assignment ya collection fully blocked rahe.

lientService.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Services/BranchAdmin/BranchFeeClientService.cs)

### 1. Student ID (`STU-xxxxx`) vs. Roll Number (`2300548`) Alignment
* **Problem**: Student page par student ka code `STU-26004` tha jabki Student Fee page par sirf Roll Number (`2300548`) dikh raha tha, jisse confusion ho raha tha.
* **Solution**:
  - [`StudentFeeDto.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Contracts/Fees/StudentFeeDto.cs) mein `StudentCode` field add kiya.
  - [`FeeMappingExtensions.cs`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Application/Features/OrgAdmin/Fees/FeeMappingExtensions.cs) mein `StudentCode = entity.Student?.StudentCode` map kar diya.
  - Dono pages ([`OrgAdmin/Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/OrgAdmin/Fees.razor) aur [`BranchAdmin/Fees.razor`](file:///d:/Rishabhks45/vargshala/src/Vargshala.Web/Components/Pages/BranchAdmin/Fees.razor)) par:
    - **Main Table**: Ab Teal badge mein Student ID (e.g. `STU-26004` / `STU-26002`) aur saath me `• Roll: 2300548` clearly dikhta hai.
    - **Assign Fee Drawer**: Dropdown me Student Name ke saath `(STU-xxxxx • Roll: xxxxx)` dikhta hai.
    - **Collect Fee Drawer & Statement Modal**: Student ID aur Roll Number dono alag-alag clearly display hote hain.
